  - *Gotchas:* Pages is in a migration/maintenance posture: Cloudflare docs actively direct users to Workers static assets ('Workers supports most Pages use cases and offers a broader feature set'; a dedicated 'Migrate From Pages' guide exists, updated 2026-10-12), so new features are landing on Workers, not Pages. Pages has no published aggregate storage cap (file-count cap instead of MB cap). To host >100 sites, Cloudflare pushes Workers for Platforms (paid product).
  - *Source:* https://developers.cloudflare.com/pages/platform/limits/
- **R2 free tier (object storage with free egress)** (high) — Free every month: 10 GB-month storage, 1 million Class A operations, 10 million Class B operations, and egress is free (R2 charges no egress at any tier). Paid Standard: $0.015/GB-month, $4.50/M Class A, $0.36/M Class B. Added 2026: R2 SQL free tier 10 GB data-scanned/mo (billing enabled 2026-08-03); R2 Data Catalog free tier 1M catalog ops + 10 GB compaction/mo (billing enabled 2026-08-03).
  - *Eligibility:* Any free Cloudflare account; no credit card required; no .edu or regional limit.
  - *How to claim:* Create an R2 bucket in the dashboard (or via `wrangler r2`); free tier is automatic — no application.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry; monthly free allowances reset. Verified on R2 pricing page last updated 2026-10-01.
  - *Gotchas:* Free tier applies to Standard storage only — Infrequent Access storage is NOT covered; R2 SQL minimum 10 MB scanned per query; unauthorized (401/403) requests are not billed; connecting R2 to other metered services may be charged by those services.
  - *Source:* https://developers.cloudflare.com/r2/pricing/
- **D1 free tier (serverless SQLite)** (high) — Free: 5 GB total storage, 5,000,000 row reads/day, 100,000 row writes/day, $0. Paid: first 25B rows/mo reads, 50M rows/mo writes, first 5 GB included, then $0.75/GB-mo storage.
  - *Eligibility:* Any free Cloudflare account; no credit card; no .edu or regional restriction.
  - *How to claim:* `wrangler d1 create my-db` or dashboard; included automatically in the Workers Free plan.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry; daily quotas reset at 00:00 UTC. Limits as of pricing page updated 2026-10-02.
  - *Gotchas:* Quotas are per-DAY, not per-month (daily reads cap = up to 150M/mo, writes = 3M/mo); D1 is designed for Workers bindings — it adds no egress cost.
  - *Source:* https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/platform/pricing/
- **Durable Objects free tier (stateful coordination)** (high) — Free: 100,000 requests/day and 13,000 GB-s of duration/day; SQLite-backed storage is the only DO storage option on the Free plan. Paid: 1M requests/mo (+$0.15/M), 400,000 GB-s/mo (+$12.50/M GB-s).
  - *Eligibility:* Any free Cloudflare account; no credit card; no .edu or regional restriction.
  - *How to claim:* Deploy a Worker with a DO class via `wrangler deploy`; free allowances automatic.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry; daily reset at 00:00 UTC.
  - *Gotchas:* Containers-backed DOs are NOT available on Free (listed N/A) — container DO billing is on the $5/mo paid plan; DO SQLite storage billing began January 2026 (target 2026-01-07 per changelog on the pricing page).
  - *Source:* https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/platform/pricing/
- **Workers KV free tier** (high) — Free: 100,000 key reads/day, 1,000 writes/deletes/lists/day, 1 GB stored data; limits reset daily at 00:00 UTC. Paid: 10M reads/mo (+$0.50/M), 1M writes (+$5.00/M), 1 GB (+$0.50/GB-mo).
  - *Eligibility:* Any free Cloudflare account; no credit card; no .edu or regional restriction.
  - *How to claim:* Create a KV namespace in the dashboard / `wrangler kv`; automatic.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry; daily reset. KV legacy API routes (/accounts/.../workers/namespaces/*) are deprecated — migrate to /storage/kv/namespaces/* before 2026-10-15.
  - *Gotchas:* The write cap (1,000/day) is the binding constraint for most apps; legacy API route deprecation deadline 2026-10-15 (past due as of 2026-10-22, so use the new routes).
  - *Source:* https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/platform/pricing/
- **Cloudflare Pages Free plan (free tier)** (high) — $0/mo, permanently free, no application. Static hosting: unlimited requests and unlimited bandwidth ('Requests to static assets are free and unlimited' — Workers platform pricing doc). Monthly/structural limits: 500 builds (deploys) per month, 1 concurrent build, 20-minute build timeout; up to 100 projects per account (limit 'not routinely increased'; new-project creation throttled for first 48h of a new account); 100 custom domains per project (free; Pro 250, Business/Enterprise 500); 20,000 files per project on Free (paid plans get 100,000 via env var PAGES_WRANGLER_MAJOR_VERSION=4); 25 MiB max per file; unlimited preview deployments; unlimited dashboard users; _headers 100 rules (2,000 chars/value); _redirects 2,100 total (2,000 static + 100 dynamic). No region limits stated in the limits doc. Docs last updated Sep 5, 2026; overview doc also states '500 deploys per month on the Free plan'.
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone with a Cloudflare account; no .edu, no company, no approval. No credit card required for the free plan in practice (dashboard sign-up is cardless) — this is not explicitly stated on the docs pages I read today, so treat the no-card sub-claim as medium confidence; the official limits/pricing docs simply never gate the Free plan on payment.
  - *How to claim:* Create a free account at dash.cloudflare.com (no card), then deploy a Pages project via Git integration, C3 CLI, or direct upload. Project lands on the Free plan automatically; add a custom domain in the project's domains tab if needed.
  - *Expiry:* Permanent free tier, no expiration or renewal. Quotas reset monthly (builds) or daily (Workers-side quotas).
  - *Gotchas:* (1) Pages Functions are billed as Workers: free Workers plan = 100,000 requests/day and 10 ms CPU time per invocation, plus free quotas for KV (100k reads/1k writes, 1 GB), D1, Queues, DO — functions that exceed these fall under the paid $5/mo minimum or overage rates. Static-asset traffic stays free and unlimited regardless. (2) Large assets (>25 MiB) don't fit — use R2 public buckets instead. (3) 100-project cap is effectively a hard limit; beyond that Cloudflare points to Workers for Platforms. (4) Strategic shift 2025–2026: Pages was folded into the Workers platform — docs now say 'Start new projects with Workers' and 'Workers supports most Pages use cases'; Pages itself is not being sunset but new features land in Workers. (5) Paid plan column in the limits doc (Pro/Business) corresponds to the Workers platform pricing (from $5/mo min) — Pages alone has no standalone paid subscription; '100 custom domains/project' on Free is per-project, multiplied across up to 100 projects.
  - *Source:* https://developers.cloudflare.com/pages/platform/limits/;https://developers.cloudflare.com/pages/;https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/platform/pricing/
- **Cloudflare for Startups (startup credit program)** (high) — Official program page read today: up to $350,000 in credits for early-stage companies, tiered: Tier 3 = $10,000 credits (bootstrapped/self-funded, under $1M raised, no minimum funding, benefits: community/Discord/docs); Tier 2 = $100,000 (under $5M raised, funded by an affiliated partner; account manager case-dependent + expert sessions); Tier 1 = $350,000 ($5M+ raised, funded by an affiliated partner; account manager, priority support, bi-weekly Solutions Architect office hours). Credits cover usage-based services incl. Pages: Workers, Workers for Platforms, Durable Objects, Workflows, Workers AI, KV, D1, Queues, Vectorize, R2 (capped $10,000), Pages, Images and Stream, Cache Reserve, Argo Smart Routing. Product caps: Workers AI $2,500 (T3)/$10,000 (T2)/$50,000 (T1); R2 $10,000; AI Gateway temporarily NOT covered. Included at no cost in all tiers: 3 enterprise domains, unlimited Business and Pro plans, DDoS/bot protection, WAF with 1,000 custom rules, SSL, CDN+DNS, LB (2 origins), Rate Limiting (1 rule), 100% SLA. Excluded from credits: registrar fees, Enterprise add-ons, Magic Transit, Enterprise Spectrum, Dedicated IPs, China Network.
  - *Eligibility:* ALL must be met: for-profit business; incorporated/founded within the last 10 years; funded up to Series B (within last 12 months); actively building a technology product; publicly accessible live website; public presence on LinkedIn, X or GitHub; first-time applicants only; business email matching your domain (no personal email). Explicitly excluded: educational institutions, personal blogs, consultancies, service-based agencies, MSPs, resellers. No .edu (students use the free plan instead). No geographic restriction stated (China Network excluded and needs a dedicated contract).
  - *How to claim:* Apply via the landing page linked from cloudflare.com/startups (form at /lp/startups): fill out the application describing product and team; review typically within 48 hours; activation confirmation by email; questions to startups@cloudflare.com. A valid payment method must be uploaded on file as part of the program.
  - *Expiry:* Credits valid 1 year or until fully consumed, whichever comes first; Cloudflare states it cannot approve extensions. After expiration, the credit card on file is billed for usage unless a prepaid contract is signed.
  - *Gotchas:* Credits are for usage-based services only — the free Pages plan is $0 to begin with, so the program only pays for your overage/paid-plan consumption; 'no cost' bundle (3 enterprise domains, WAF, SLA) is the real structural win. CREDIT CARD / payment method IS required (unlike the free plan). Funding caps matter: Tier 1 needs an affiliated-partner round, not just any $5M+. The older regional sub-pages (en-gb/en-ca /forstartups/) still show the pre-2026 four-tier $5k/$25k/$100k/$250k structure — the main cloudflare.com/startups page is authoritative.
  - *Source:* https://www.cloudflare.com/startups/
- **Student / education offer** (medium) — No student-specific free offer exists for Pages or the Cloudflare developer platform. The official education page (read today) lists no academic license, no student credits, and no .edu-based benefit; the only quantified education program is 'Project Cybersafe Schools': free cloud email security and DNS filtering (CIPA-compliant) for small US K-12 public school districts — a security offering, not hosting, and not available to individual students/educators. Cloudflare's startup program explicitly excludes educational institutions. Individual students are served by the universal $0 Pages Free plan (no card, no application), which is the same as the general public's free tier; free developer courses/community exist but carry no hosting entitlement.
  - *Eligibility:* Students: no dedicated eligibility — they use the general Free plan (any account, no card). Project Cybersafe Schools: small US public K-12 districts. No .edu requirement anywhere; .edu email grants no special Cloudflare benefit.
  - *How to claim:* Nothing to claim for students beyond the standard free plan. For K-12 districts: contact Cloudflare education team via the contact form on cloudflare.com/education.
  - *Expiry:* N/A — absence of a student program; free plan is permanent.
  - *Gotchas:* Search budget in this session was exhausted, so I could not exhaustively rule out ad-hoc student partnerships (e.g., accelerator/course-specific credit drops like the historical Workers Launchpad, which is partner-gated, not student-gated). Confidence is medium: I read the official education page today but student programs, if any, could live under partner/launchpad names I didn't fetch.
  - *Source:* https://www.cloudflare.com/education/
- **Cloudflare Free Plan (CDN + DDoS + DNS + basic WAF)** (high) — Perpetual $0/month free tier. Includes global CDN, always-on unlimited DDoS mitigation (no configuration), unlimited DNS zones/queries, TLS certificates, and basic WAF + Bot Fight Mode on the free tier. Runs on 100+ data centers across 330+ cities. Bundles the Workers Free compute tier and per-service free allowances (R2 10 GB, KV 1 GB, D1 5 GB, Durable Objects, Hyperdrive).
  - *Eligibility:* Open to anyone (students, startups, individuals, all regions). No .edu email, no age/funding cap. A credit card is NOT required: a Free account is created with just email + password.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Sign up at dash.cloudflare.com/sign-up with email + password (no CC). 2) Add your domain via CNAME or point name servers to Cloudflare. 3) CDN + DDoS protection auto-activate for free.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry — active for the life of the account.
  - *Gotchas:* No CC needed to start, but adding a payment method unlocks paid products and any overage/egress beyond the free allowances is billed. Free plan has per-feature caps (e.g. Pages 500 builds/mo and 100 custom domains; 512 MB CDN cache).
  - *Source:* https://www.cloudflare.com/plans/
- **Cloudflare Workers Free Plan (Compute)** (high) — $0. 100,000 Worker invocations/day (resets 00:00 UTC), 10 ms CPU per request, up to 100 Workers, 64 MiB memory, 50 external + 1,000 Cloudflare-service subrequests per invocation. Free allowances also include KV 1 GB, R2 10 GB, D1 5 GB, Durable Objects, Hyperdrive 100k queries/day.
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone on a Free Cloudflare account.
  - *How to claim:* Create a free account, then deploy a Worker with `npx wrangler deploy`. No credit card needed.
  - *Expiry:* Daily limits reset each day; plan is perpetual.
  - *Gotchas:* The task's 'known so far' figure of ~1M requests/day is OUTDATED — the official current free limit is 100,000/day. Hitting the cap returns Error 1027 unless you upgrade to Workers Paid ($5/mo min).
  - *Source:* https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/platform/limits/
- **Cloudflare Observability / Monitoring / Uptime free tier** (high) — Shared Observability free allowance of 0.5 GB/day ingestion + 7-day retention, pooling Traces, Workers Logs, Real User Monitoring (RUM), AI Gateway logs, R2 access logs, and Issues. Workers Logs free tier = 200,000 events/day (3-day retention). Cloudflare Radar and RUM are available on the Free plan for monitoring/uptime-style visibility.
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone on the Free plan; no CC or .edu required.
  - *How to claim:* In the dashboard, enable Analytics & Logs / Real User Monitoring; free to ingest up to 0.5 GB/day.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry; daily ingestion resets at 00:00 UTC.
  - *Gotchas:* When the 0.5 GB/day cap is hit, ingestion stops until the reset. Scheduled change: from Dec 1, 2026, Workers Logs moves to Cloudflare Observability pricing — plan retention/limits may change after that date.
  - *Source:* https://developers.cloudflare.com/observability/pricing/
- **Cloudflare Startup Program (up to $350,000 in credits)** (high) — 3 tiers (2026): Tier 3 = $10k (bootstrapped/self-funded, under $1M raised, no minimum funding); Tier 2 = $100k (under $5M raised, funded by an affiliated partner); Tier 1 = $350k ($5M+ raised, affiliated partner). Credits apply to usage-based services (Workers, KV, D1, Queues, Vectorize, R2 up to $10k, Workers AI up to $50k, Images, Stream, Argo, Cache Reserve). Included at NO COST on every tier: 3 enterprise domains, unlimited Business/Pro plans, DDoS + bot protection, WAF with 1,000 custom rules, CDN + DNS, load balancing, 100% SLA.
  - *Eligibility:* For-profit startup founded within the last 10 years, funded up to Series B within the last 12 months, actively building a tech product, with a public verifiable website, active on LinkedIn/X/GitHub, first-time applicant, and a business-domain email. Since Sep 2025, non-profit/civil-society/public-interest orgs are also eligible (up to $250k).
  - *How to claim:* Apply at cloudflare.com/startups (form describing the product + team). You must create a Cloudflare account AND add a valid payment method on file (used to bill overages). Review takes up to ~48 hours; approval arrives by email.
  - *Expiry:* Credits valid for 1 year or until fully consumed, whichever is first. No extensions; upgrading within a cohort does not reset the 12-month window.
  - *Gotchas:* Unlike the plain Free plan, the Startup program REQUIRES a payment method on file. Product-specific caps: R2 up to $10k; Workers AI $2.5k (Tier 3)/$10k (Tier 2)/$50k (Tier 1); AI Gateway temporarily NOT covered. 'Funded up to Series B within last 12 months' means recently-fundraised startups only.
  - *Source:* https://www.cloudflare.com/startups/
- **Cloudflare Academy — free courses & certification (education license)** (medium) — Self-paced, free online training curriculum plus free certification exams (e.g. Cloudflare Certified Associate). No .edu email, no student status, and no funding cap required — open to anyone. Educators can also run the material in classrooms; instructors can be supported through Cloudflare's instructor program.
  - *Eligibility:* Open to anyone (students, educators, self-learners, all regions).
  - *How to claim:* Go to Cloudflare Academy, create a free account, and start any course for $0.
  - *Expiry:* Courses/certs do not expire; content updates over time.
  - *Gotchas:* Could not re-fetch the Academy page today (DNS resolution failure for academy.cloudflare.com from this environment), so the exact current course catalog/URL was not re-verified. The free/open/self-paced model and free-certification positioning are consistent with Cloudflare's learning/community pages but were not re-read on the live page.
  - *Source:* https://academy.cloudflare.com/
- **Cloudflare Ambassador & Community programs (student leaders)** (high) — The Cloudflare Community Ambassador program explicitly targets 'student leaders' (alongside community organizers and technical creators): run on-campus clubs, meetups, workshops, and content. Benefits include Cloudflare credits, marketing assets, swag, and access to Cloudflare experts; cohorts last 1 year with the option to reapply. A separate Community Engineer track provides discretionary grants for meaningful open-source work. Neither is a paid role.
  - *Eligibility:* Open application; non-technical enthusiasts welcome; student club leaders, community organizers, and open-source contributors.
  - *How to claim:* Apply through cloudflare.com/community under the Ambassador (or Community Engineer) track.
  - *Expiry:* Cohort-based: 1-year Ambassador cohorts, reapply to continue; Community Engineer has no fixed term.
  - *Gotchas:* Credits/grants are discretionary (awards, not a flat entitlement) and cohort-based, not a guaranteed free tier like the Free plan.
  - *Source:* https://www.cloudflare.com/community/
- **Cloudflare Free (Network & CDN) plan — always-free** (high) — $0/month website/CDN plan: CDN, unmetered DDoS protection, Universal SSL, WAF with Free Managed Ruleset, DNS, SSO, Role-based Account Control; 70 WAF rules + 10K bulk redirects; bot mitigation limited to 'Simple bots'; community forums support only. Paid tiers for comparison: Pro $20/mo annual ($25 monthly), Business $200/mo annual ($250 monthly), Enterprise custom. Page targets 'personal or hobby projects'.
  - *Eligibility:* Open to anyone worldwide; no .edu email, no student status, no credit card/payment method required for the Free plan (sign up at dash.cloudflare.com/sign-up, 'Start building for free' — payment method only needed later to enable paid products/services).
  - *How to claim:* Create a free Cloudflare account (no payment method), add your domain as a zone, set nameservers to Cloudflare. Instant, perpetual, renews monthly at $0. The plan page (network-cdn) does not state a hard zone/site cap or bandwidth cap; Free has historically been unlimited-bandwidth with all sites/zones included.
  - *Expiry:* Permanent; no expiry. Free-plan feature sets can shrink/change with plan revisions.
  - *Gotchas:* Only 'Simple bots' mitigation and 70 rules on Free; WAF max payload and bot analytics are paid-plan features. Paid add-ons cost extra (e.g. Log Explorer $1/GB after 10 GB).
  - *Source:* https://www.cloudflare.com/plans/network-cdn/
- **Cloudflare Developer Free plan — Workers, R2, D1, KV, Pages, AI (perpetual, not a trial)** (high) — Perpetual free allowances (monthly unless noted): Workers 100,000 requests/day + 10 ms CPU/request (128 MB memory; 100 Workers; 5 cron triggers; 50 external subrequests/request); R2 10 GB-month Standard storage + 1M Class A + 10M Class B ops/month; D1 5 GB total storage + 5M rows read/day; Workers KV 1 GB storage + 100K reads/day; Pages 500 builds/month, 100 custom domains/project, 20K files, 20-min build timeout; Workers AI 10,000 neurons/day; Observability free: 0.5 GB/day ingestion, 7-day retention; Workflows 3,000 steps/day + 1 GB-month storage. No charge for egress/bandwidth on Workers and R2. Workers Paid plan is $5/mo minimum (includes 10M requests + 30M CPU ms).
  - *Eligibility:* Open to anyone; no .edu/student requirement, no funding/company status, and credit card NOT required to create a Free account — docs explicitly frame adding a payment method as a post-signup step to 'enable additional products and services'.
  - *How to claim:* Sign up free at dash.cloudflare.com/sign-up; free allowances are included automatically on any new account — no application, no key, no card. Exceeding Workers quotas returns error 1027 (fail-open/closed), not a bill, on the Free plan; overages on R2/D1/KV etc. do bill, which requires a payment method on the account.
  - *Expiry:* Permanent free tier; daily limits reset at midnight UTC, monthly allowances reset each billing month. 2026 additions started charging for some add-ons (see sunset_notes).
  - *Gotchas:* R2 free tier covers Standard storage only (Infrequent Access is never free). New metered add-ons (R2 SQL, R2 Data Catalog billed 2026-08-03; AI Search billing from 2026-11-01 with 5M ingestion tokens, 10 GB-month, 1K semantic + 1K full-text queries/month included) can produce invoices on an otherwise-free account — monitor 'Billable Usage' and set spend caps. Workers AI Gateway is 'temporarily not covered' by startup credits.
  - *Source:* https://www.cloudflare.com/plans/
- **Cloudflare Startup Program — annual credits ($10K / $100K / $350K tiers)** (high) — Cloudflare's startup program (cloudflare.com/startup, apply at /lp/startups) offers credits valid 1 year or until consumed (extensions not granted). Tiers recently raised: Tier 3 $10,000/yr (was $5,000), 'no minimum funding required'; Tier 2 $100,000/yr + account manager; Tier 1 $350,000/yr (was $250,000) + Office Hours + priority support. Exclusions/caps: R2 capped at $10K credit; Workers AI capped at $2.5K/$10K/$50K by tier; AI Gateway temporarily not covered; domain registrar fees and premium network services (Magic Transit, Enterprise Spectrum, Dedicated IPs, China Network) excluded. Review typically ~48 hours, confirmation by email.
  - *Eligibility:* For-profit company only; incorporated no more than 10 years ago; funded up to Series B (funding within last 12 months, except Tier 3 needs no minimum funding); business email on the company domain — personal emails are rejected, so .edu/student emails are NOT eligible; must have a public website and public LinkedIn/X/GitHub presence; first-time applicant only; no region limit is stated on the page.
  - *How to claim:* Apply via the form at https://www.cloudflare.com/lp/startups with company/business-domain email; if accepted you receive the tier's annual credit to offset Cloudflare spend. The startup program is for companies, so students/universities/sole .edu users effectively cannot claim it.
  - *Expiry:* Credits: 12 months from grant or until consumed, whichever first; no extensions. Active members near old caps may email startups@cloudflare.com for case-by-case tier upgrade (does not reset the 12-month window).
  - *Gotchas:* Business-entity + company-domain email rules make it unusable for individual students, classes, or nonprofit/university projects; 'funding within last 12 months' + up-to-Series-B cap means late-stage and >10-year-old companies are out; entry tier is now $10K not the $5K referenced in older sources.
  - *Source:* https://www.cloudflare.com/startup/
- **Education / .edu programs — no dedicated student or university program found** (medium) — No .edu-only or student-exclusive Cloudflare offer was found today: no Cloudflare education license, no student credits, no university pricing page (cloudflare.com/academy and /lp/startups-education equivalents 404; academy.cloudflare.com did not resolve in this session; Cloudflare docs contain no 'academy/education' content). Cloudflare Academy (free courses/certifications for IT/security training, open to everyone — not .edu-gated) has existed in recent years, but its current status is unconfirmed from today's fetches. The only 'free' education-adjacent value is the general free developer plan plus free Radar data dashboards.
  - *Eligibility:* n/a — negative finding: no .edu/student-email program exists to claim; free platform is available to students exactly as to anyone else (no card required).
  - *How to claim:* Nothing to claim. Students get the standard Free developer plan (Workers 100K req/day, R2 10 GB, D1 5 GB, KV 1 GB, Pages 500 builds/month) with no special student terms; universities would have to use the startup program only if they fit its for-profit company rules, which they typically do not.
  - *Expiry:* n/a
  - *Gotchas:* If Cloudflare Academy has moved hosts (dead subdomain in this environment), re-verify before citing its free courses/certifications; treat any .edu-only claim as unsubstantiated.
  - *Source:* https://academy.cloudflare.com/(DNSdidnotresolve,2026-10-22)
- **2025–26 change:** Changes in 2025–2026: (1) The Cloudflare for Startups program was restructured from the April 2025 four-tier layout ($5k/$25k/$100k/$250k, per blog post 'Startup Program update') to the current three-tier layout ($10k/$100k/$350k) — Tier 3 raised $5k→$10k and top tier $250k→$350k; regional pages (e.g. cloudflare.com/en-in/forstartups) still display the outdated $250k/4-tier structure, so use the US .com page as authoritative. (2) Sept 22, 2025 (Birthday Week 2025): Students program launched (12 months free Workers Paid for US .edu 18+) and the program was extended to non-profits/civil-society orgs (up to $250k credits). (3) Sept 2025: 'Come build with us' hubs — program participants get office access in SF/Austin/London/Lisbon from Jan 2026. (4) Sept 28, 2026: 'The Cold Start' competition announced — 5 early-stage companies pitch at Cloudflare Connect; grand prize $500,000 in credits (competition, not a standing program). (5) Workers AI pricing page (last updated Oct 20, 2026) moved to per-model granular unit-based pricing display while backend billing remains Neuron-based; 8 frontier models are now gated behind a paid billing method. Sunset of 2022–2024 programs: the original $1.25B/$2B-pledge Workers Launchpad funding program (2022) evolved into the current cohort Launchpad; no free-offer sunsets this cycle — all five offers above remain active as of 2026-10-22. Note: one follow-up web search (non-profit program page URL) could not be executed because the session's WebSearch budget was exhausted; non-profit and Launchpad findings lean on blog/press summaries and cached page content, hence medium confidence.
- **2025–26 change:** 2025–2026 program changes: (1) The Startup Program was restructured in 2025 from the April 2025 4-tier structure ($5,000 / $25,000 / $100,000 / $250,000, 'founded within last 5 years') to the current 3 tiers ($10k / $100k / $350k, founded within last 10 years) — entry tier doubled, top tier raised $250k->$350k, and the $25k tier was dropped. Members on the old caps can request a case-by-case upgrade that does not reset their 12-month expiry. (2) Sept 22, 2025: program expanded to non-profit, civil-society, and public-interest organizations (up to $250k credits; first application window ran until Dec 1, 2025). (3) Sept 22, 2025: Cloudflare for Students launched (12-month free paid developer features, US .edu only) — an education license/credit that did not exist before 2025. (4) 'The Cold Start' ($500k credit prize) closed with its Oct 19, 2026 Connect event; applications ended Oct 2, 2026. (5) Stale pages: some localized pages (e.g., cloudflare.com/en-in/forstartups/) still show the old '5 years / $5k-$250k' criteria — treat as outdated; the global /startups/ page is authoritative. (6) Community note: a global Cloudflare outage on Nov 18, 2025 (Workers KV, Access, Turnstile, dashboard, core CDN) is a recurring single-vendor risk with free tiers. The pre-launch claim 'WAF (1,000 rules) on all [startup] tiers' is confirmed — but the regular Free plan only gets 5 WAF custom rules; 1,000 is the Enterprise-plan limit that the startup program includes at no cost.
- **2025–26 change:** As of 2026-10-22, Cloudflare has NO distinct free offers targeted at students or startups: (1) No startup program — the official partners page (cloudflare.com/partners, fetched 2026-10-22) lists only PowerUP channel program, technology alliances, global SIs, service providers, and the Cloudflare@OCI alliance; no startup track, no free credits, no age/funding-capped program. (2) No trial credits — paid Workers plans simply start at $5/mo with usage-based overage; no free-trial-of-paid credits are offered. (3) No education license/.edu discount for infrastructure — cloudflare.com/plans (fetched 2026-10-22) shows usage-based Free/Paid tiers only and mentions no education, student, or nonprofit discount; no .edu email is required for any tier; the free tiers are universally available to any account. Cloudflare's only education-adjacent offering is free learning content/certification programs (Learning Hub / CF-ICD), which is training, not hosting/PaaS — and its landing page (developers.cloudflare.com/learning-hub) was 404, so status is unverified. 2025–2026 material changes affecting the free tiers: Pages is not sunset but is being pushed into a migrate-to-Workers posture (a 'Migrate From Pages' guide and a Pages index note steering users to Workers static assets; Pages limits unchanged: 500 builds/mo, 100 projects, 20,000 files); Workers Logs free tier migrates to Cloudflare Observability pricing on 2026-12-01; Durable Objects SQLite storage billing begins January 2026; legacy KV API routes deprecated with 2026-10-15 migration deadline; R2 free tier (10 GB) unchanged, with new free sub-tiers added in 2026 for R2 SQL (10 GB scanned/mo) and R2 Data Catalog (1M ops + 10 GB compaction/mo), both billing-enabled 2026-08-03. Note: the WebSearch budget was exhausted for this session, so 2025-2026 change verification relied on official docs/changelog pages (fetched today) rather than press/news coverage; 'last updated' dates on the source pages are 2026, confirming the limits above are current.
- **2025–26 change:** 2024-2026 changes: (1) Startup program was revamped Sep 2024 from a one-size $500k-era offer to a credits system capped at $250,000 (3 tiers). April 11, 2025: expanded to four tiers — $5,000 (bootstrapped/stealth, founded last 5 yrs), $25,000 (funded to $1M), $100,000 ($1M-$5M + approved VC/accelerator), $250,000 (Tier-1 VCs / AI / Workers Launchpad) — with a 5-year founding window and a 'BOOTSTRAPPED' promo-code path. The current 2026 page replaced this with three tiers ($10k/$100k/$350k), a 10-year founding window, 'funded up to Series B within the last 12 months', a hard payment-method-on-file requirement, and per-tier Workers AI caps. Old $250k/$5k-$250k four-tier copy still lingers on stale localized sub-pages (en-gb, en-ca) — treat cloudflare.com/startups as current. (2) Pages platform shift 2025-2026: Pages was consolidated into the Workers platform; docs now steer new projects to Workers ('Start new projects with Workers'; Pages Functions billed exactly as Workers), but Pages is not sunset — Free plan numbers stand as verified Sep 5, 2026 doc: $0, unlimited static requests/bandwidth, 500 builds/mo, 100 projects, 100 custom domains/project, 20,000 files (raised from the prior 10,000 cap; paid tiers unlock 100,000 via PAGES_WRANGLER_MAJOR_VERSION=4), 25 MiB max asset. (3) No student/education program was created or sunset in this window; 'Project Cybersafe Schools' (free K-12 email security + DNS filtering, US districts) remains the only education-facing free offer. No trial-credit program (e.g., a $500 API-credit trial) is currently advertised on the official pages I read.
- **2025–26 change:** Material 2025–2026 changes: (1) Startup Program tier structure was revamped repeatedly: Sept 2024 revamp set the ceiling at $250k; April 2025 expanded to 4 tiers ($5k/$25k/$100k/$250k); by mid-2026 it was restructured into 3 tiers ($10k/$100k/$350k, per the current cloudflare.com/startups page and a May 2026 announcement). (2) Sep 2025: non-profit, civil-society, and public-interest organizations became eligible for the Startup program (up to $250k) — application window opened with a Dec 1 close for that cohort. (3) Eligibility window widened from 'founded within last 5 years' (2019–2024) to 'founded within last 10 years.' (4) Workers free-plan subrequest limit changed Feb 2026: now 50 external + 1,000 Cloudflare-service subrequests per invocation (previously a flat 1,000 total). (5) Workers Logs: effective Dec 1, 2026 it moves to Cloudflare Observability pricing (a near-term change affecting the free observability front). (6) Sep 2026: Free-account creation expanded so users can create up to 5 standalone Free accounts via dashboard or API. No traditional 'education license discount' or separate individual developer-credit grant exists at Cloudflare; the free value for students is the perpetual Free plan + free Academy + discretionary Ambassador/Community grants, and for startups the credits program.
- **2025–26 change:** Material 2025-2026 changes: (1) Startup program tiers were "recently updated" (undated on page, current as of 2026-10-22): Tier 3 raised $5,000→$10,000/yr and Tier 1 $250,000→$350,000/yr — older references to a "$5K credit" are stale; entry tier is now $10K. (2) No general-public time-boxed trial or one-off trial-credit offer exists; the only dollar-credit offer is the application-based startup program (perpetual free tiers are not trials and never expire). (3) New billable dimensions landed on the free platform in 2026: R2 SQL and R2 Data Catalog billing enabled 2026-08-03 for non-Enterprise accounts (free tier 10 GB scanned / 1M catalog ops / 10 GB compaction per month), AI Search billing begins 2026-11-01 (free: 5M ingestion tokens, 10 GB-month, 1K semantic + 1K full-text queries/month), and 2026-02-11 raised Workers subrequest limits (Free stays at 50 external/1,000 internal per invocation; Paid default 10,000, configurable to 10M) — free accounts can still accrue charges on metered add-ons, so set spend limits. (4) 2025-12-05 WAF max-payload change: Free zones already protected to 1 MB; paid plans need support ticket to enable. (5) Unbound/Bundled Workers plans are deprecated and unavailable to new accounts. (6) Education: no .edu-only program found and Cloudflare Academy's host (academy.cloudflare.com) did not resolve today — treat its current availability as unverified; if it was sunset in 2025-2026, no student/education free offer remains at Cloudflare.

### Datadog Free Plan

- **Infrastructure Free Plan (permanent free tier, Monitoring front)** (high) — $0/month free tier of Infrastructure Monitoring: up to 5 hosts, 1-day metric retention, unlimited users, unlimited alerts, 5 containers per host, 100 custom metrics per host, 500 custom events per host, 1,000+ integrations; includes dashboards, host/container maps, SAML SSO, outlier/anomaly detection, Watchdog insights. Shown on the main pricing page as Infrastructure 'Free' plan: 'Starting At $0', 'Up to 5 hosts', '1-day metric retention'.
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone; self-service account creation, no credit card required. Data-housing region chosen at signup and cannot be migrated later.
  - *How to claim:* Sign up at datadoghq.com (Start free trial / Get started free flow), install the Datadog Agent on up to 5 hosts; account lands on the Free plan at no cost.
  - *Expiry:* Indefinite; no sunset. 1-day retention and per-host allotments are the standing caps.
  - *Gotchas:* Not the 14-day trial: the free plan is separate and permanent. 1-day metric retention vs 15 months on paid/student accounts; no public free allowance for Uptime/Synthetics quotas appears on the current pricing page; exceeding allotments (hosts, custom metrics/events, containers) requires a paid plan; 'unlimited users' but agents on the 5 free hosts only.
  - *Source:* https://www.datadoghq.com/pricing/
- **14-day free trial of the full Datadog platform** (high) — 14 days of 'unlimited monitoring' of the entire Datadog platform (metrics, traces, logs, 1,000+ integrations, alerting incl. anomaly/outlier/forecast). 'No credit card required' (stated repeatedly). Signup requires a business email; form collects password, name, job title, company, optional phone, and data-housing region.
  - *Eligibility:* Open to all companies (the DDFS FAQ says the 2-week trial is 'available to all companies'); business email expected; no credit card; no startup/student restriction.
  - *How to claim:* Go to datadoghq.com/free-datadog-trial/, complete the signup form, agent installs and trial is active immediately; DDFS applicants are explicitly told to sign up for the trial while their startup application is under review.
  - *Expiry:* 14 days; no auto-conversion or renewal terms are published on the page; after expiry the account is on the paid plan or the free plan.
  - *Gotchas:* Trial is NOT the free plan and cannot be converted into the student program ('We cannot transition users, agents, hosts, or metrics' per the student program). Region choice at signup is not migratable. Startup-program applicants use this same trial as their pre-approval onboarding.
  - *Source:* https://www.datadoghq.com/free-datadog-trial/
- **Datadog for Startups (DDFS) - up to $100k credits, one year** (high) — 'A year of Datadog Pro, up to $100k in credits' for the first year; covers the complete platform - 'all Generally Available (GA) products' plus new features that GA, with preview products usable 'if space permits' (no watered-down tiers). Acceptance email typically within 3-5 business days. Credits applied to the account after approval.
  - *Eligibility:* Series A or earlier (no dollar funding cap published); must be new to Datadog (no current or prior Datadog customer, never received DDFS credits); must apply through an official referral partner (VCs, hyperscaler and vendor partner startup programs) - no direct application without a referral.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Submit application at datadoghq.com/partner/datadog-for-startups/ (startup email: startups@datadoghq.com); 2) Sign up for a Datadog trial and use it while the application is reviewed; 3) Receive acceptance by email (3-5 business days); 4) Credits are applied - 'start building'. Activation must be confirmed within 30 days of acceptance or the acceptance expires (may reapply later if eligible).
  - *Expiry:* Credits for the first year only; no renewal route stated. Inactivity: a warning is sent after 3 months unused, and after another 30 days without use the company is removed from the program.
  - *Gotchas:* No referral partner = ineligible; the fallback is the 14-day trial. Excludes anyone who was ever a Datadog customer or prior DDFS recipient. No credit card/ payment details appear in the application flow. The 30-day activation window and inactivity removal are the main traps.
  - *Source:* https://www.datadoghq.com/partner/datadog-for-startups/
- **Student Developer program (education license via GitHub Student Developer Pack)** (high) — Free Datadog Pro account with a monthly allocation: 10 hosts (Infrastructure and APM), 500 GB ingested logs, 50 containers, 10 lambda functions, 5 Fargate tasks, 1,000 browser tests, 10,000 API tests, 1,000 Browser RUM sessions, 1,000 Mobile RUM sessions, 100,000 LLM requests; standard and custom metric retention up to 15 months; alerts, users, and Datadog Support access included. 'Free Datadog up to 2 years', available while the participant remains an active GitHub Student Developer Pack member; Datadog will renew the account as long as pack membership continues.
  - *Eligibility:* Currently enrolled students who are members of the GitHub Student Developer Pack (GitHub handles enrollment verification - .edu email/student ID on GitHub's side; Datadog's own page does not impose a separate .edu requirement). Not for commercial projects. All accounts are created in the US5 region only.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Log in with GitHub at studentpack.datadoghq.com ('Log in to Github to get started'); 2) the account is auto-provisioned in the US5 region; 3) log in at us5.datadoghq.com and configure your Datadog Agent with the issued API keys. Faculty/curriculum access: studentpack@datadoghq.com.
  - *Expiry:* Up to 2 years, renewable while enrolled in the GitHub Student Developer Pack; if a term lapses Datadog will extend/renew active pack members.
  - *Gotchas:* US5 region only - student accounts cannot be migrated to another region. Cannot be created by converting an existing free-trial account. A temporary 'staff user' is created for provisioning and disabled after the invite. Commercial use is prohibited. LLM/RUM/browser quotas are monthly, not one-off.
  - *Source:* https://studentpack.datadoghq.com/
- **Agent Observability Free tier (Observability front)** (high) — Free tier of Agent Observability: '$0 per month', up to 40K LLM spans/month, 15-day trace retention, unlimited context & evals, full feature access; targeted at 'individuals and small teams'. Also on the main pricing page: Feature Flags are free for usage below 1 million monthly flag configuration requests (no plan purchase needed for sub-1M MFCRs).
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone - no plan purchase, no startup/student requirement; a Datadog account (trial or free) is enough.
  - *How to claim:* Sign up at datadoghq.com; Agent Observability 'is already available in your account and free to start using' - no separate application.
  - *Expiry:* Permanent free tier; monthly 40K LLM span cap and 15-day trace retention are the standing limits.
  - *Gotchas:* 15-day trace retention vs 15 months on paid/student accounts; 40K LLM span/month cap; this is per-user/small-team oriented - startup teams usually get far more via DDFS or the 14-day trial.
  - *Source:* https://www.datadoghq.com/pricing/
- **Time-limited promo: Bits Testing free through Nov 30 (AI credits, Observability front)** (high) — On the current pricing page, 'Bits Testing' (AI-powered autonomous test generation inside Synthetic Monitoring) is listed as 'Free of charge through Nov 30' (2026). A free in-browser trial of Bits Testing also exists at bits-testing.datadoghq.com ('Try free in browser').
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone with a Datadog account; not student/startup-specific.
  - *How to claim:* No application; use Bits Testing in a Synthetics workflow; in-browser demo requires no account at all.
  - *Expiry:* Promotion ends Nov 30 (page language 'through Nov 30'); after that Bits Testing reverts to AI-credits-based pricing.
  - *Gotchas:* Short-dated promo - verify the exact Nov 30 year (page fetched 2026-10-22, so Nov 30, 2026). Not part of any student/startup program; counts against AI credits after the promo ends.
  - *Source:* https://www.datadoghq.com/pricing/
- **No distinct public free Uptime Monitoring or CDN offer found (Uptime & CDN front)** (medium) — Uptime Monitoring is now folded into Synthetic Monitoring under Digital Experience: the Synthetic Monitoring product page states 'Usage-based pricing per test run, with no per-user fees' and no public free endpoint/check-per-minute quotas or free test types are published on any current Datadog page I read today. The RUM page advertises only the 14-day trial. There is no Datadog CDN product in the current product index, Digital Experience pages, or docs navigation - Datadog's 'edge' offering is Edge Device Monitoring (IoT), which has no free tier.
  - *Eligibility:* n/a - nothing to claim; the front has no standalone free offer as of 2026-10-22.
  - *How to claim:* n/a
  - *Expiry:* n/a
  - *Gotchas:* Absence of published free uptime quotas may partly reflect client-side-rendered pricing; legacy pages historically showed a free uptime endpoint allowance on the Infrastructure Free plan, which is no longer displayed. If you need free uptime, the practical options are the 14-day trial (unlimited) or the 5-host Free plan, but no free uptime endpoint count is publicly stated today.
  - *Source:* https://www.datadoghq.com/products/digital-experience/synthetic-monitoring/

### Elastic Observability

- **Elastic Cloud 14-day free trial (Observability incl. logs, APM, metrics, traces, RUM, synthetics/uptime)** (high) — 14-day trial of Elastic Cloud (Hosted and Serverless) covering the full Elastic Observability suite: log monitoring, APM, infrastructure monitoring, AIOps/generative AI, digital experience monitoring (RUM, synthetics, uptime), OpenTelemetry, plus AutoOps. Official pricing FAQ states the trial 'includes a cluster with 8GB of RAM and 240GB of storage'; the Observability trial page says 'Get up to 360GB of storage during your trial' (figures differ between pages). No credit card required to sign up; after the trial you only pay if you add a card, pay-per-use with no long-term commitments.
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone, any email — no .edu, no company stage, no funding cap, no region limit (deploys in AWS/Azure/GCP regions; note sign-ups routed through Azure/GCP marketplaces get the 7-day marketplace trial instead).
  - *How to claim:* 1) Sign up at cloud.elastic.co (email/Google/Microsoft), 2) choose a cloud provider, 3) create a deployment — up and running in <5 minutes. Trial clock starts when the first cluster is created.
  - *Expiry:* 14 days from cluster creation. No extension or renewal; deployments/data persist afterward but using them beyond the trial requires adding a credit card.
  - *Gotchas:* Trial is not free-forever: post-trial, adding a card converts you to a paid subscription and billing begins. Official pages disagree on storage cap (240GB in pricing FAQ vs 'up to 360GB' on trial page) — actual limits may vary by provider/region. Azure/GCP-marketplace sign-ups get a 7-day trial, not 14.
  - *Source:* https://www.elastic.co/cloud/cloud-trial-overview;https://www.elastic.co/cloud/observability-trial-overview;https://www.elastic.co/pricing/faq
- **AWS Marketplace / GCP Marketplace 7-day trials (no credit card to Elastic)** (medium) — Subscribing via AWS Marketplace gives a 7-day free trial (single deployment, not charged for first 7 days) during which you automatically get an Enterprise-level subscription; you pay Elastic via your AWS bill with no separate card to Elastic. GCP Marketplace: 7-day trial, one deployment plus three projects; usage-based billing starts automatically after 7 days; one Elastic Cloud org per GCP billing account; if you unsubscribe, the trial ends immediately and cannot be resumed even after resubscribing.
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone with an AWS or GCP account (billing account used for the marketplace purchase). No .edu or startup requirement.
  - *How to claim:* AWS: Elastic Cloud on AWS Marketplace > View purchase options > sign in > use; billing starts automatically after day 7 unless you delete deployments. GCP: subscribe on the GCP Marketplace Elastic page, link/create an Elastic Cloud account, verify email; delete resources to stop charges.
  - *Expiry:* 7 days; automatic conversion to usage-based billing on the marketplace bill (Elastic Cloud billed through AWS/GCP, egress/snapshot charges metered in US East for AWS).
  - *Gotchas:* Billing starts automatically after 7 days — delete deployments to avoid charges. These are separate from the 14-day direct trial; a marketplace subscription replaces/links to the trial account, it does not extend it. AWS Marketplace charges metered in us-east-1 regardless of deployment region.
  - *Source:* https://www.elastic.co/docs/deploy-manage/deploy/elastic-cloud/aws-marketplace;https://www.elastic.co/docs/deploy-manage/deploy/elastic-cloud/google-cloud-platform-marketplace
- **Self-managed Elastic Stack (Elasticsearch/Logstash/Kibana/Beats) — free 'Basic' license, perpetual** (high) — The Elastic Stack (Elasticsearch, Kibana, Beats, Logstash) can be downloaded and run for free, forever: 'the OSS features as well as the free Basic features of our software at no cost', including native security features, role-based access control, and true Kibana multitenancy. Elastic explicitly says it is free 'to learn, to develop, and to run production at scale'. No trial, no credit card, no cap. (Note: since the 2021 re-licensing, the stack is under Elastic License v2 / SSPL rather than Apache-2.0, and 'free' = the Basic feature set.)
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone, including students and startups; no .edu, no region or usage limit.
  - *How to claim:* Download the Elastic Stack from elastic.co/downloads and self-host on your own infrastructure (containers, VMs, on-prem).
  - *Expiry:* Perpetual — no expiry, no renewal; free Basic features have no stated time limit.
  - *Gotchas:* 'Free ELK' ≠ full feature set: APM scale-out, many log-scaling/security (SIEM/XDR) and ML capabilities sit in paid Gold/Platinum/Enterprise tiers; Basic has no vendor support. Budget for your own infrastructure and operations; self-hosting is free but not costless. License is ELv2/SSPL, not classic Apache OSS.
  - *Source:* https://www.elastic.co/pricing/faq
- **Elastic Cloud Serverless: built-in free allowances (50 GB/month egress, 10k free workflow/agent executions) with 14-day trial — no perpetual free tier** (high) — There is no free-forever tier for Elastic Cloud Serverless; instead the pay-as-you-go plans include standing free allowances: 50 GB free egress per month (then $0.05/GB), 10,000 free workflow executions (then ~$0.0108/execution), and 10,000 free Agent Builder executions (then ~$0.025/execution). Below that, logs/traces ingest starts ~$0.07/GB, metrics ingest ~$0.023–0.09/GB, retention ~$0.005–0.019/GB-month. Higher support tiers add 5–15% of the bill. A 14-day free trial of Serverless is also available (serverless-registration); uptime monitoring in production is paid: synthetic browser tests $0.0123/test run, lightweight ping $28.00/location/month.
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone with a free Elastic Cloud account; no .edu or startup requirement; pay-as-you-go with no minimum commitment. New serverless metrics pricing effective July 1, 2026.
  - *How to claim:* Sign up via cloud.elastic.co/serverless-registration (or a 14-day trial), create a serverless project; free allowances apply automatically each month — no voucher or form.
  - *Expiry:* Free allowances recur monthly (egress allowance resets per billing month). Trial itself is 14 days; allowances have no stated sunset.
  - *Gotchas:* Not a free tier — every GB ingested/retained above the free egress/execution allowances bills automatically. Uptime monitoring is essentially not free at scale (ping locations are the costliest add-on at $28/location/month). No credit card needed for trial, but continuing serverless usage beyond the trial requires billing info.
  - *Source:* https://www.elastic.co/pricing/serverless-observability
- **Up to $5,000 Elastic Cloud credits via AWS Activate (self-founded startups: $1,000) — not offered on elastic.co itself** (medium) — Elastic's startup credits are distributed through AWS Activate, not Elastic's own site: 'Up to $5,000 in Elastic Cloud credits for AWS Activate members' covering search, observability (logs, metrics, traces) and security, incl. serverless; a separate channel line lists '$1,000 in Elastic Cloud credits for Self Founded Startups' (per Superscout, last checked 2026-09-29; the $5K figure was confirmed in an official AWS Startups LinkedIn post, 2026-09-01, describing August 2026 Activate vendor offers).
  - *Eligibility:* Startups: AWS Activate membership (funded/accelerator path for up to $5K; 'Self Founded' path for $1K). No .edu requirement. Application-based, not open to everyone.
  - *How to claim:* Apply through the AWS Activate Perks page (aws.amazon.com/startups/perks, now serving at startups.aws.com) — the Elastic entry requires an application through the AWS channel.
  - *Expiry:* No published expiration; perk amounts/terms set by AWS Elastic partnership (aggregator last-checked dates: 2026-09-29).
  - *Gotchas:* Elastic's own site publishes no startup program (elastic.co/startups is a 404; StartupPerks verified 2026-10-07 that the $5,000 figure appears only on third-party lists). The AWS Perks page returned 404 to direct fetches (likely JS-rendered), so exact current amounts should be re-verified on the AWS page or via AWS startup support before relying on them. Credits apply to Elastic Cloud (not self-managed licenses).
  - *Source:* https://superscout.co/perk/elastic;https://www.linkedin.com/posts/aws-startups_every-month-we-add-exclusive-offers-to-invoke-activity-7500537957607440386-uq7R(AWSStartupspost,2026-09-01)
- **Elastic education/training: free self-paced courses and a free GenAI Associate exam (no .edu product licenses, no exam vouchers found)** (medium) — Elastic's training hub (elastic.co/training) offers 'Learn Elastic for free' self-paced modules at no cost (e.g., 'Getting started: What is Elastic?', 'RAG foundation', 'Semantic search foundation', 'Intro to MCP with Elasticsearch MCP Server'). The 'GenAI Associate Accreditation Exam' is listed with a direct free 'Take exam' link. Instructor-led courses (Elastic Observability Engineer, Elasticsearch Engineer, Kibana Data Analysis, Security for SIEM) carry no visible student discount or voucher on the page; standard certification exam pricing and voucher programs are not published on the site. There is no .edu-based product licensing program found — students get the same free Basic self-hosted stack and 14-day trial as everyone else.
  - *Eligibility:* Open to anyone; no .edu email, no regional limit, no cost for the free modules/exam. No student-specific tiers or discounts found.
  - *How to claim:* Browse elastic.co/training, click 'Start module' for free courses; 'Take exam' link on the GenAI Associate exam; register a free account if prompted.
  - *Expiry:* Free modules are standing; the free GenAI exam is promotional and may change without notice. No product offer has expiry.
  - *Gotchas:* Free exam appears limited to the GenAI Associate accreditation — other certifications (Engineer-level) are paid; don't assume all exams are free. No academic software license exists; 'education license' for Elastic Cloud does not appear anywhere on elastic.co as of 2026-10-22.
  - *Source:* https://www.elastic.co/training
- **2025–26 change:** 1) Elastic's own 'Elastic for Startups' program (announced ~2021–2022: Elastic Cloud credits + developer-advocate support, targeting seed/pre-Series A) no longer exists on elastic.co — /startups returns 404 and a 2026-10-07 check of elastic.co (StartupPerks) found no startup program in Elastic's sitemaps; the 'up to $5,000' figure surviving in aggregators traces to the AWS Activate channel, not to any Elastic-published program. The current official route for startup credits is AWS Activate (up to $5K funded / $1K self-founded, per AWS Startups' own 2026-09-01 announcement). 2) No perpetual free cloud tier ever existed for Elastic Cloud — the only standing free offers are the 14-day trial, marketplace 7-day trials, serverless free allowances (egress/executions), and the free self-hosted Basic license. 3) Pricing-page changes in the 2025–2026 window: Elastic Cloud Serverless is now GA (AWS/GCP/Azure) with pay-as-you-go or prepaid models, and new serverless metrics pricing took effect July 1, 2026; the classic 'Essentials' entry tier is no longer listed — current subscription tiers are Standard/Gold/Platinum/Enterprise. 4) Trial terms stable: still 14 days, no credit card required. 5) Gotcha for students specifically: Elastic does not publish any .edu verification, academic license, or student voucher program — students and startups get the same offers as general consumers plus the AWS Activate credit channel for startups.

### Grafana Labs

- **Grafana Cloud Free tier (free forever, no credit card)** (high) — Official Grafana Cloud Free plan, read today (2026-10-22) on grafana.com/pricing and grafana.com/products/cloud/free-tier. Exact limits: 10k active metric series/month; 50 GB logs/month; 50 GB traces/month; 50 GB profiles/month; k6 limited to 500 virtual user hours/month; Synthetics (uptime) limited to 100k API test executions + 10k browser test executions/month; Frontend Observability 50k sessions/month; Grafana Assistant 3 active AI users (40M tokens/user/month) plus 25M tokens/month for service-account usage; Grafana Visualization 3 active users; IRM 3 active users; 14-day retention for metrics, logs, traces, profiles & k6 tests; community support. 'Free forever', 'No charges ever', 'No credit card required' — stated on each service block. Note: the commonly cited '100K k6 executions' is outdated; k6 is now capped in virtual user hours, while the 100k figure applies to Synthetics executions.
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone — no .edu email, no student status, no startup status, and no credit card required. Free tier page markets it as 'perfect for personal projects, exploring new ideas, and early-stage startups'. No region restriction mentioned; stacks can be created in Grafana's public-cloud regions. Total user count is capped at 3 active users on the free tier.
  - *How to claim:* Sign up free at grafana.com (Create free account → grafana.com/auth/sign-up/create-user/?cta=free). Create an organization and a stack in your chosen region; all services above are available within the stated limits at $0. No credit card or payment method is asked. Upgrade to Pro (from $19/month + usage, 13-month metric retention / 30-day log-trace retention) or Enterprise (from $25,000/year spend commit) anytime if you outgrow the limits.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry or sunset stated; marketed as 'Free forever'. Limits shown are current as of 2026-10-22.
  - *Gotchas:* 1) Total seat cap of 3 active users — a team larger than 3 cannot all use the free tier. 2) As of April 20, 2026, Grafana disabled alerting and reporting for NEWLY created free/trial stacks (email alerts/reports restricted to users inside your own instance; Cloud Alertmanager access disabled) as an anti-abuse measure — existing free accounts keep full functionality (third-party mirror of the Grafana status incident; medium confidence). 3) As of Jan 1, 2026, Grafana Assistant became billed on active users; free plan includes 3 AI users with a 40M tokens/user/month message cap, and paid plans charge $20 per additional active user beyond 3. 4) Free tier data is 14-day retention only; exceeding volume limits means downsampled/dropped data or upgrade — no egress-charge surprise, but no billing above limits on free (hard caps, not overage billing). 5) 'Adaptive Telemetry' and AI features included on free are allotment-limited, not unlimited.
  - *Source:* https://grafana.com/pricing/(limitstableread2026-10-22;corroboratedbyhttps://grafana.com/products/cloud/free-tier/andhttps://grafana.com/auth/sign-up/create-user/?cta=free&pg=graf&plcmt=pricing-card)
- **Grafana Labs Startup Program (up to $100,000 in Grafana Cloud credits)** (high) — Announced at ObservabilityCON on 2024-09-25; program page still live as of 2026-10-22 (© 2026 footer, ObservabilityCON 2026 banner; terms mirror the original launch). Awards up to $100,000 in Grafana Cloud credits, valid for 12 months or until the startup's next funding round (whichever comes first). Credits apply to ANY Grafana Cloud service: managed metrics/logs/traces backends, k6 performance testing, profiling, incident response & management, frontend/application observability, and synthetic monitoring. Additional perks for accepted startups: all Enterprise data source plugins, 24x7 Grafana support, guaranteed 20% discount off list price after credits or the 12-month window end (plus further consumption-based discounts), and access to Grafana's integrations team.
  - *Eligibility:* Open to all new customers — explicitly including existing Grafana Cloud Free-account holders and Grafana OSS users migrating to managed cloud; students with a startup can apply too. Ideal profile: raised less than $10 million and under 25 employees, but the page says 'apply even if you do not meet these guidelines'. No stated company-age cap, no .edu requirement, no region limit. Admission is at Grafana Labs' sole discretion (not first-come-first-served). Accepted startups must agree to be a reference customer and may be asked to participate in marketing (guest blogs, event speaking). No credit card required to apply.
  - *How to claim:* Fill out the 4-question application form: https://grafana-labs.typeform.com/to/cSI7ohks (linked from the official blog post). Wait for Grafana Labs' discretionary decision; if accepted you receive the credit allocation plus the perks listed above.
  - *Expiry:* No published sunset date as of 2026-10-22. Each award lasts 12 months or until the company's next funding round; the guaranteed 20% discount continues after credits end. No 2025-2026 amendments to the program structure are stated on the page.
  - *Gotchas:* 1) Discretionary selection — 'apply' does not mean 'get in'; no SLA on decisions. 2) Obliges reference-customer and marketing commitments. 3) Credits offset usage-based billing; they are not free hosting — if usage exceeds the credit value you must pay (at the 20% discount). 4) Program is startup-company-oriented, so a solo student project with no company may be a weak application. 5) The $100k figure is 'up to' — actual credit amounts vary per acceptance.
  - *Source:* https://grafana.com/blog/grafana-labs-startup-program/(read2026-10-22)
- **Student / education license — no dedicated Grafana education offer exists** (medium) — No Grafana student program, .edu-only license, or university discount program was found as of 2026-10-22: grafana.com/education/ and grafana.com/academy/ both return HTTP 404; the official free-tier and pricing pages contain no education/student section; the free tier's stated audience is 'personal projects, exploring new ideas, and early-stage startups' — students simply use the same no-credit-card free tier as everyone else. (Grafana's free learning content exists under its learning hub/docs, but it grants no cloud credits or .edu-verified accounts.)
  - *Eligibility:* N/A as a standalone program. The de facto student path is the general free tier: no .edu email required, no credit card, up to 3 users, 14-day retention.
  - *How to claim:* Students should just create a free Grafana Cloud account at grafana.com (no verification email or .edu address gate) and use it within free-tier limits; for a student-founded company, also consider the Startup Program application.
  - *Expiry:* Absence-of-program determination as of 2026-10-22; web search was unavailable (session search budget exhausted) so this is based on the official pages fetched today plus 404 probes, not an exhaustive web sweep.
  - *Gotchas:* Do not confuse Grafana Academy/courses (free learning content, if they reappear under another URL) with a cloud-credit student offer. If a student program launches later, it would appear on grafana.com.
  - *Source:* https://grafana.com/products/cloud/free-tier/(noeducationmention,read2026-10-22)+404probesofhttps://grafana.com/education/andhttps://grafana.com/academy/
- **Trial / trial-credit offers — no separate trial-credit program found; a 'Trial' tier exists alongside Free** (low) ⚠ low confidence: verify before claiming — As of 2026-10-22 the official pricing page lists no paid-plan trial-credit program for students or startups, and the sign-up page pushes 'The (actually useful) free forever plan' (create free account) rather than a time-boxed trial. The April 2026 status incident separately references a Grafana 'Trial' tier/stack category next to 'Free' (both affected by the new-stack alerting restriction), indicating a trial offering of some kind exists in the product, but Grafana's public pages reviewed today do not document trial-credit amounts, durations, or eligibility. The Startup Program (finding 2) is the only structured 'credit' offer.
  - *Eligibility:* Unknown/not documented publicly; no .edu or startup gating documented for any trial credits.
  - *How to claim:* No documented claim path; students/startups should rely on the free tier plus the Startup Program application. Worth re-checking grafana.com/pricing when search is available.
  - *Expiry:* N/A — based on absence of documentation as of 2026-10-22.
  - *Gotchas:* Absence of a documented trial-credit program is a snapshot observation; Grafana's 'Send more, charge more is broken. We are changing it.' pricing banner on the pricing page signals an in-flight pricing overhaul, so tier structure (including any trial) may change in 2026.
  - *Source:* https://grafana.com/pricing/(notrial-creditmention,read2026-10-22);trial-tierexistencecorroboratedbytheApril2026statusincidentsummary(https://isdown.app/status/grafana/incidents/573697-restrictions-on-alerts-reports-for-grafana-cloud-free-users)

### Healthchecks.io Free Plan

- **Hosted Hobbyist Free Plan (permanent free tier)** (high) — The hosted service's Hobbyist plan is $0/month with no time limit: monitor 20 jobs (cron jobs / heartbeats), 100 log entries kept per job (oldest entries evicted when the cap is hit — a job pinging every 5 minutes retains only ~8 hours of history; a weekly job retains ~2 years). No SMS/WhatsApp/phone-call credits on the free tier; email, webhook, Slack, Discord, Telegram, Pushover, Signal, ntfy, Matrix, GitHub Issues and other web-based alert channels work. Paid tiers for comparison: Supporter $5/mo (same limits, donation only), Business $20/mo (100 jobs, 1000 log entries, 50 SMS/WhatsApp + 20 phone credits, email support; $16/mo billed annually), Business Plus $80/mo (1000 jobs, 500 SMS/WhatsApp + 100 phone credits, priority support; $64/mo annual, 20% annual discount).
  - *Eligibility:* Open to anyone (including students and startups): sign up with just an email address via magic-link; no credit card, no .edu address, no region restriction, no funding or company-age criteria. Not a trial — a permanently free tier. Running multiple accounts to exceed the 20-job cap is explicitly labeled abuse by Healthchecks.io.
  - *How to claim:* Go to healthchecks.io, click Create Free Account / sign up for the Hobbyist plan with an email address (magic link), no payment details. Then create up to 20 checks (ping URLs or cron expressions) and have your job curl the ping URL on each run.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry; free forever. Author has publicly stated (July 2024 blog post) that he is not planning to tighten free-plan limits or raise paid prices.
  - *Gotchas:* No phone/SMS/WhatsApp alerts on the free tier (paid only). 100-entry log cap means short retention for fast jobs. Inactive accounts receive 'Inactive Account Notification' emails; long inactivity may affect the account. Capping out at 20 jobs means upgrading to Business at $20/mo. Payments when you do pay run via Braintree (PayPal division); no card data stored on Healthchecks.io servers.
  - *Source:* https://healthchecks.io/pricing
- **Self-Hosted Open-Source Healthchecks (free, unlimited)** (high) — Healthchecks is open source under the BSD license (code on GitHub, developed in the open). Self-hosting the full product on your own infrastructure is free with no check-count limits — 'unlimited checks on your own infrastructure', which is the offer referenced for students building on cheap/homelab or university cloud credits. You run the entire service (web app + PostgreSQL + object storage for ping bodies) and every feature is available, minus the hosted service's ops guarantees (Hetzner bare metal, hot standby, daily S3 backups).
  - *Eligibility:* Open to anyone — no account, no credit card, no .edu, no region limit. Costs shift to you: you need hosting, and you become the operator (backups, upgrades, failover). The about page states plainly: 'Healthchecks is free software, and can be self-hosted.'
  - *How to claim:* Clone the BSD-licensed GitHub repo (github.com/healthchecks/healthchecks), run the Django app + PostgreSQL per its docs, point your cron jobs at your own instance's ping URLs.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry — BSD license is permanent; the project is accepting contributions and the author (one-person SIA Monkey See Monkey Do, Latvia) keeps it maintained as of 2026 (11 years in business per the About page).
  - *Gotchas:* You pay for your own infrastructure and all ops burden (database failover is manual even on the hosted version, and a single operator handles it); no 'free' in the sense of zero cost — only zero license cost. Feature parity with the hosted version can lag or differ; you don't get the hosted reliability track record (99.9%+ since 2015).
  - *Source:* https://healthchecks.io/about/
- **Free Business Plan for Open-Source Projects and Nonprofits** (high) — Healthchecks.io offers the Business plan free of charge for open-source projects and nonprofits — worth $20/month — including 100 monitored jobs, 1000 log entries per job, 50 SMS/WhatsApp credits and 20 phone-call credits, plus email support. It is not a student or startup program; the eligible categories are OSS projects and nonprofits. The About page tracks ~106 such accounts (OSS + nonprofit) vs ~1,236 paying.
  - *Eligibility:* Open-source projects and registered nonprofits only. No .edu email, no student license, and no dedicated startup program exists on the official pages. No published funding caps or age criteria; eligibility is judged by the team on request. No region limit stated. No credit card required (plan is free).
  - *How to claim:* Email contact@healthchecks.io (listed in the FAQ) asking for the free Business plan as an open-source project or nonprofit; they set the account up. It is not self-serve — you must ask.
  - *Expiry:* No published expiry or renewal date; it is a standing program (listed on the pricing-page FAQ and FAQ as of today, 2026-10-22).
  - *Gotchas:* Not available to for-profit startups or individual students — claiming it as a commercial startup would be misusing the program. It's manual/e-mail based with no SLA on response; the Free tier (20 jobs) already covers most personal/student use cases. SMS/WhatsApp/phone credits are consumed by alert volume; when they run out you'd need a paid upgrade to top up.
  - *Source:* https://healthchecks.io/faq/

### Honeycomb Free Plan

- **Honeycomb Free plan (free forever)** (high) — Free plan verified on today's pricing page: up to 20M events/month AND up to 100M metrics (time-series) data points/month, 2 triggers, 0 SLOs, no Service Map. Included features: Distributed Tracing, BubbleUp, OpenTelemetry support, Team Query History, query result permalinks, Honeycomb Metrics, Canvas AI Copilot, Hosted MCP, Agent Timeline, Honeycomb Intelligence. Marketing states 'Unlimited seats' and 'Unlimited querying'. No trace-count cap, dashboard cap, or retention window is printed on the pricing page today; the 60-day high-resolution retention figure is the historical figure from the plan's docs (not re-verified today, docs were restructured). Optional add-on Telemetry Pipeline (extra ingest, OTel processing) starts at $0.10/GB; Pro starts at $150/month for 50M events (up to 750M).
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone (no .edu, no startup criteria, no funding cap). No region limits stated. No credit-card requirement is stated anywhere on the pricing page (unconfirmed; page is silent).
  - *How to claim:* Sign up at https://ui.honeycomb.io/signup ('Start for free') — free plan is the default plan; no sales gate on this tier. Then send events via OTel SDKs or API key.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry — explicitly 'free forever'. Upgrade trigger: exceeding 20M events/month (or 100M data points) requires Pro, starting at $150/month.
  - *Gotchas:* Genuinely free tier, not a time-boxed trial. Only 2 triggers free (no SLOs, no Service Map) — alerting beyond 2 triggers is paid. Retention not printed on today's pricing page (60-day figure carried over from historical plan docs, unconfirmed). Optional Telemetry Pipeline add-on is metered at $0.10/GB and adds cost on top of free ingest. CC requirement unverified without running the signup flow.
  - *Source:* https://www.honeycomb.io/pricing
- **Honeycomb for Builders — Pro Bronze (academic / nonprofit / open-source)** (high) — Official builders program (verified today on honeycomb.io/honeycomb-for-builders): 'Pro500 for qualifying open source, non-profit, and academic projects in perpetuity' — complimentary Pro plan at the Pro500 volume tier (≈ up to 500M events/month; Pro's max tier per pricing page is 750M, so Pro500/Pro750 denote event-volume tiers, not seats). Includes all Pro features, community onboarding sessions, office hours, and Honeycomb swag. Free in perpetuity.
  - *Eligibility:* Academic projects/organizations, non-profits, and open-source projects. No .edu-email requirement published; eligibility is vendor-determined via application. Individual students are covered only through their academic organization or OSS project — there is no student-specific tier.
  - *How to claim:* Apply via 'Talk to us' on https://www.honeycomb.io/honeycomb-for-builders (routes to /get-a-demo; third-party listings say the application form is embedded on that page). No published review timeline; approval at vendor discretion.
  - *Expiry:* In perpetuity for qualifying projects — no renewal trigger or end date published; implicitly revocable if the project stops qualifying.
  - *Gotchas:* The tier is for projects/organizations, not named individuals — a student must apply on behalf of their academic org or OSS project. Third-party directories note award timing/duration are not published. Pro500's ~500M events/month allowance dwarfs the free tier — most valuable academic offer.
  - *Source:* https://www.honeycomb.io/honeycomb-for-builders
- **Honeycomb for Builders — Pro Silver (early-stage startup discount)** (high) — Official builders program (verified today): 'Pro750 at 50% off for 1 year, available to early stage startups (Pre-seed, Seed round, Early series A)'. Pro750 = Pro plan at the 750M events/month volume tier (Pro's top tier; base Pro starts at $150/month for 50M events, so Pro750 is a high-volume custom-priced configuration — 50% discount applies to that configuration, absolute $ not public). Includes all Pro features, onboarding sessions, office hours, swag.
  - *Eligibility:* Startups at Pre-seed, Seed, or Early Series A stage; no age cap published beyond funding stage. No accelerator requirement at this tier. A third-party directory (StartupCreds) notes bootstrapped founders as eligible — not stated on Honeycomb's own page.
  - *How to claim:* Same application path: 'Talk to us' form at https://www.honeycomb.io/honeycomb-for-builders. Vendor-side approval; not self-serve.
  - *Expiry:* 1 year at 50% off; no renewal/extension terms published — after 12 months presumably full (custom) Pro pricing unless re-approved.
  - *Gotchas:* Discount, not free — do not confuse with Pro Gold. Pro750 list price is not public, so '50% off' cannot be converted to dollars from public pages. No numeric funding cap (e.g., <$X raised) stated beyond the 'Early series A' ceiling.
  - *Source:* https://www.honeycomb.io/honeycomb-for-builders
- **Honeycomb for Builders — Pro Gold (accelerator startup free year)** (high) — Official builders program (verified today): 'Pro750 free for 1 year for qualifying startups', two required conditions: (1) 'Active in a recognized startup accelerator (including Y Combinator and accelerators supporting underrepresented founders)' and (2) 'Actively using or building with OpenTelemetry'. = full Pro plan at the 750M events/month volume tier at $0 for 12 months, plus all Pro features, community onboarding sessions, office hours, swag. No purchase or commitment required.
  - *Eligibility:* Startup must (a) be active in a recognized accelerator — Y Combinator explicitly named, plus accelerators supporting underrepresented founders — and (b) actively use or build with OpenTelemetry. No .edu requirement, no separate funding cap published. YC's own 'YC AI Stack' free-credits pack (Fall 2025, $25k+ in credits) does NOT include Honeycomb — YC teams still apply via the builders form; nothing auto-redeems through deals.ycombinator.com.
  - *How to claim:* Apply via the 'Talk to us' form at https://www.honeycomb.io/honeycomb-for-builders; approval vendor-side. Third-party trackers (Sourcey) list it open to applicants meeting both conditions, no payment required.
  - *Expiry:* Free for exactly 1 year. No renewal, extension, or post-expiry pricing terms published; team expected to convert to paid Pro after expiry.
  - *Gotchas:* OpenTelemetry usage is a hard condition — teams not instrumenting with OTel are ineligible. 1-year term with no published renewal path. One third-party directory marked the offer 'stale' in a crawl, but the official page still lists it as of today — treat the official page as authoritative.
  - *Source:* https://www.honeycomb.io/honeycomb-for-builders

### Metabase Community Edition

- **Metabase Open Source / Community Edition — free, unlimited, self-hosted** (high) — Perpetually free and unlimited: unlimited users, unlimited questions & dashboards, 20+ supported data sources, MCP server, AI on your own model, unlimited transforms when self-hosted. License is AGPL (GitHub repo lists AGPL + commercial MCL files). No sign-up, no credit card, no region limits. You pay only for your own infrastructure (Docker/JAR on your host). No support, backups, or managed upgrades — those are what the paid/cloud tiers sell.
  - *Eligibility:* Everyone — no .edu email, no startup-age or funding-cap requirement, no application process.
  - *How to claim:* Self-service: deploy the official Docker image from Docker Hub or download the JAR and point it at your database (metabase.com/start/oss). Alternatively 'Get started' on the homepage routes to the paid cloud checkout at store.metabase.com/checkout.
  - *Expiry:* None — perpetual, no renewal, no sunset date stated.
  - *Gotchas:* Free edition excludes commercial features (SSO, required 2FA, row/column permissions, auditing, white-label embedding are Pro/Enterprise-only). No included support: 'All of Metabase's plans include support' means support is a paid-bundle item; self-hosters use community forums/discourse. Hidden cost is your hosting, not Metabase. AI is 'on your preferred model' (BYO) in the OSS path, so LLM API costs fall on you.
  - *Source:* https://www.metabase.com/pricing/(corroboratedbyhttps://www.metabase.com/start/ossandhttps://github.com/metabase/metabase)
- **Metabase Cloud 14-day free trial (any plan: Starter / Pro / Enterprise)** (high) — 14-day free trial of Metabase Cloud, available on any plan. Starter is $100/mo ($90/mo billed yearly, $1,080/yr; 5 users incl., extras $6/user/mo); Pro is $575/mo ($517.50/mo yearly, $6,210/yr; 10 users incl., extras $12/user/mo); Enterprise custom, 'from $20,000/yr'. Cloud trial includes automatic backups/restore/upgrades, managed PostgreSQL (AWS RDS), preconfigured SMTP, SSL, SOC 2 Type II, multi-zone HA, unlimited engineer support by email. Homepage CTA: 'Try Metabase Cloud free'.
